1	name: Deploy Landing Page to IPFS
2	
3	on:
4	  push:
5	    branches: [main]
6	    paths:
7	      - 'landing/**'
8	      - '.github/workflows/deploy-landing.yml'
9	  workflow_dispatch:
10	
11	permissions: {}
12	
13	jobs:
14	  build-and-deploy:
15	    name: Build & Pin to IPFS
16	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
17	    environment: staging
18	    permissions:
19	      contents: read
20	    steps:
21	      - uses: actions/checkout@df4cb1c069e1874edd31b4311f1884172cec0e10 # v6.0.3
22	
23	      - uses: actions/setup-node@48b55a011bda9f5d6aeb4c2d9c7362e8dae4041e # v6.4.0
24	        with:
25	          node-version: 22
26	          cache: 'npm'
27	          cache-dependency-path: landing/package-lock.json
28	
29	      - name: Install and build
30	        working-directory: landing
31	        run: |
32	          npm ci
33	          npm run build
34	
35	      - name: Upload dist to VPS
36	        uses: appleboy/scp-action@ff85246acaad7bdce478db94a363cd2bf7c90345 # v1.0.0
37	        with:
38	          host: ${{ vars.STAGING_HOST }}
39	          username: ${{ vars.STAGING_USER }}
40	          key: ${{ secrets.STAGING_SSH_KEY }}
41	          source: 'landing/dist/*'
42	          target: /tmp/cipherbox-landing
43	          strip_components: 2
44	
45	      - name: Pin to IPFS via Kubo
46	        id: ipfs
47	        uses: appleboy/ssh-action@0ff4204d59e8e51228ff73bce53f80d53301dee2 # v1.2.5
48	        with:
49	          host: ${{ vars.STAGING_HOST }}
50	          username: ${{ vars.STAGING_USER }}
51	          key: ${{ secrets.STAGING_SSH_KEY }}
52	          capture_stdout: true
53	          script: |
54	            set -euo pipefail
55	            # Kubo runs as a Docker container on the staging host (compose service
56	            # "ipfs", image ipfs/kubo:v0.40.0) — there is no host-level ipfs CLI, so a
57	            # bare `ipfs` here fails with "command not found". Run it inside the container.
58	            # Capture the container list first, THEN take the first line: piping
59	            # `docker ps` straight into `head` can SIGPIPE docker ps (exit 141) under
60	            # `pipefail` and abort before the empty-KUBO guard runs.
61	            KUBO_IDS=$(docker ps -q --filter ancestor=ipfs/kubo:v0.40.0)
62	            KUBO=$(printf '%s\n' "${KUBO_IDS}" | head -n1)
63	            if [ -z "${KUBO}" ]; then
64	              echo "::error::No running ipfs/kubo container found on staging host"
65	              exit 1
66	            fi
67	            # The SCP step landed the build at host /tmp/cipherbox-landing; the container
68	            # has no /tmp bind-mount, so copy it in before adding. Redirect docker cp's
69	            # stdout to stderr so capture_stdout returns ONLY the CID.
70	            docker cp /tmp/cipherbox-landing "${KUBO}:/tmp/cipherbox-landing" 1>&2
71	            CID=$(docker exec "${KUBO}" ipfs add -Qr --pin /tmp/cipherbox-landing)
72	            # Emit the CID immediately — the pin is already committed to Kubo, so a failure
73	            # in the best-effort cleanup below must never drop it from stdout (that would
74	            # fail the step and leave DNSLink pointing at the now-stale CID).
75	            echo "${CID}"
76	            docker exec "${KUBO}" rm -rf /tmp/cipherbox-landing || true
77	            rm -rf /tmp/cipherbox-landing || true
78	
79	      - name: Validate CID
80	        run: |
81	          CID="${{ steps.ipfs.outputs.stdout }}"
82	          if [ -z "${CID}" ]; then
83	            echo "::error::No CID returned from IPFS pin step"
84	            exit 1
85	          fi
86	          echo "CID=${CID}" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
87	          echo "Pinned landing page to IPFS: ${CID}"
88	
89	      - name: Update DNSLink
90	        if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
91	        env:
92	          CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
93	          CLOUDFLARE_ZONE_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ZONE_ID }}
94	          DNSLINK_RECORD_ID: ${{ secrets.DNSLINK_RECORD_ID }}
95	        run: |
96	          curl -sS -X PUT \
97	            "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/zones/${CLOUDFLARE_ZONE_ID}/dns_records/${DNSLINK_RECORD_ID}" \
98	            -H "Authorization: Bearer ${CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN}" \
99	            -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
100	            --data "{\"type\":\"TXT\",\"name\":\"_dnslink.cipherbox.cc\",\"content\":\"dnslink=/ipfs/${CID}\"}"
101	
102	          echo "Updated DNSLink to /ipfs/${CID}"
103	
104	      - name: Summary
105	        if: always()
106	        run: |
107	          {
108	            echo "## Landing Page Deployed"
109	            echo "- **CID:** ${CID}"
110	            echo "- **IPFS Gateway:** https://ipfs.io/ipfs/${CID}"
111	            echo "- **Commit:** ${{ github.sha }}"
112	          } >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
113	
```

> AGENT

Confirmed the bug and the fix. The `Pin to IPFS` step already redirects noise to stderr and emits only the CID, but `appleboy/ssh-action`'s `capture_stdout` **appends the drone-ssh success banner**, so `steps.ipfs.outputs.stdout` is multi-line — and `echo "CID=${multiline}" >> $GITHUB_ENV` is what throws `Value cannot be null`. The pin itself works every time; only the CID capture is broken.

This is unrelated to Phase 58 and lives on `main`, so I'll fix it on its own branch + PR. Let me set that up off `origin/main`.

A bare multi-line value breaks `>> \"$GITHUB_ENV\"` with\n          # \"Unable to process file command 'env' … Value cannot be null. > AGENT

All extraction cases pass (CIDv0, CIDv1, empty-guard) and `actionlint` is clean. Bonus: passing the step output via `env:` instead of inline `${{ }}` in `run:` is exactly the pattern zizmor wants, so this also removes a template-injection flag. Let me commit, push, and open the PR.

{
  "command": "cd /Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box\ngit add .github/workflows/deploy-landing.yml\ntimeout 90 git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nci: extract IPFS CID from ssh-action stdout in landing deploy\n\nThe Deploy Landing Page to IPFS workflow fails on every push to main at the\n\"Validate CID\" step with \"Unable to process file command 'env' … Value cannot\nbe null. (Parameter 'name')\". The pin itself always succeeds.\n\nRoot cause: appleboy/ssh-action's capture_stdout appends the drone-ssh banner\n(\"✅ Successfully executed commands to all hosts\") to the remote stdout, so\nsteps.ipfs.outputs.stdout is multi-line even though the remote script echoes\nonly the CID. `echo \"CID=${multiline}\" >> \"$GITHUB_ENV\"` then writes a bare\nmulti-line value, which $GITHUB_ENV rejects (it requires heredoc delimiters for\nmulti-line values).\n\nExtract just the IPFS CID (CIDv0 Qm…/CIDv1 baf…) from the captured output with\ngrep before writing it to $GITHUB_ENV, and pass the raw stdout via env rather\nthan inline ${{ }} interpolation in run (avoids shell/script injection — also\nclears the zizmor template-injection flag).\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -4\ngit push -u origin ci/landing-deploy-cid 2>&1 | tail -5",
  "description": "Commit and push the landing deploy fix",
  "dangerouslyDisableSandbox": true
}

(Parameter 'name')\n```\n\nThe IPFS pin itself always succeeds (the CID is produced and committed to Kubo on the staging host) — only the capture of the CID into `$GITHUB_ENV` fails, which then aborts the DNSLink update.\n\n## Root cause\n\n`appleboy/ssh-action`'s `capture_stdout` appends the drone-ssh banner to the remote stdout:\n\n```\nREDACTED\n===============================================\n✅ Successfully executed commands to all hosts.\n===============================================\n```\n\nSo `steps.ipfs.outputs.stdout` is **multi-line** even though the remote script `echo`s only the CID (and redirects `docker cp` noise to stderr). `echo \"CID=${multiline}\" >> \"$GITHUB_ENV\"` writes a bare multi-line value, and `$GITHUB_ENV` rejects it — multi-line values require heredoc delimiter syntax.\n\n## Fix\n\nIn the `Validate CID` step:\n\n- Extract just the IPFS CID (CIDv0 `Qm…` / CIDv1 `baf…`) from the captured output with `grep -oE -m1` before writing to `$GITHUB_ENV`.\n- Pass the raw stdout via `env:` instead of inline `${{ }}` interpolation in `run:` — avoids shell/script injection and clears the zizmor `template-injection` flag.\n\nNo change to the remote pin script or any other step.\n\n## Testing\n\nLocally validated the extraction against the real failing output:\n\n- CIDv0 multi-line stdout → extracts `Qmf6j47…` ✓\n- CIDv1 (`bafy…`) multi-line stdout → extracts the `baf…` CID ✓\n- empty stdout → extraction empty, existing `No CID returned` guard fires ✓\n- `actionlint .github/workflows/deploy-landing.yml` → clean ✓\n\nThe deploy only runs on `main` (or manual `workflow_dispatch`), so end-to-end verification happens on merge; the next push to `main` touching `landing/**` should deploy cleanly.\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n\nenv -u GITHUB_TOKEN gh pr create \\\n  --base main \\\n  --head ci/landing-deploy-cid \\\n  --title \"ci: extract IPFS CID from ssh-action stdout in landing deploy\" \\\n  --body-file /tmp/landing-pr-body.md 2>&1 | tail -5",
  "description": "Open PR for the landing deploy fix",
  "dangerouslyDisableSandbox": true
}

And the `sequence binding mismatch` error count in all three desktop logs is now **0** (was 100+). The first-publish skew fix works.

### Summary of this session's two threads

**1. Phase 58 desktop-E2E regression (PR #544)**
- Root cause: my Phase 58 resolve-side sequence binding used strict `embedded == DB`, stricter than the publish-side D-09 gate — first-publish file records embed IPNS-native `0` while the API stores DB `sequenceNumber=1`.
- Fixed both bindings (Rust `verify.rs` + sdk-core) to accept the one documented skew, returned the DB-authoritative seq, added a cross-language vector + Rust/JS unit tests. Commits `f759c4a90` (fix) + `fc0c5a754` (follow-up todo), pushed. Local + CI E2E green.
- Cargo.lock: the `0.7.0` increment is committed on the branch and in sync.

**2. Landing page deploy failure on main → PR #546** ([link](https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/pull/546))
- `appleboy/ssh-action`'s `capture_stdout` appended the drone-ssh banner, making `outputs.stdout` multi-line, which broke `>> $GITHUB_ENV`. The pin itself always succeeded.
- Fixed by extracting just the CID via `grep` and passing stdout through `env:` (also clears a zizmor template-injection flag). Verified extraction (CIDv0/v1/empty-guard) + `actionlint` clean.

**3. main's stale Cargo.lock** — ignored per your call (workspace-member version field only; #544 self-corrects it on merge).

Both PRs are ready for your review/merge. Merge decisions are yours — I haven't merged either.
